# Next TODO

> Status: Living tracker. Use the latest entries as the source of truth, and assume older bullets may describe superseded intermediate states.


## Goal

这份清单记录当前工作 backlog。它不是默认 onboarding 文档；接手项目时应先读 `docs/INDEX.md` 和 `../PROGRESS.md`，再回到这里看待办。

## Session Resume Rule

- 每次 context compact / 会话恢复后，先检查最近交互记录与 `PROGRESS.md` 的最新条目，再开始执行命令或重新排查。
- 开始跑命令前，再额外确认两件事：
  - 当前 `workdir` 是否已经回到真实仓库根目录
  - Python / 测试命令是否显式走仓库 `venv`（当前默认应为 `./.venv-tests/bin/python`）
- 目标是先恢复“上一次做到哪里、哪些结论已确认、哪些命令已经跑过”，避免重复做同一轮环境探测、回归或根因分析。

## Project Framing For New Sessions

- 这个仓库默认应描述为招聘自动化、公开资料补全、候选人研究和 provider 驱动的工作流产品化。
- 总结项目时优先使用：
  - 公开信息补全
  - 候选人和公司 roster
  - 后台恢复 / 队列治理 / 预算控制
  - 证据质量 / 人工确认 / 可审计导出
- 必要的 provider 名称、配置键、API contract、CLI flag 需要保持精确；但如果只是做项目总结或 handoff，不要脱离业务上下文地放大高敏感术语。

## 2026-06-07 Stage Checkpoint

- [x] **Local asset / Target Candidates shell cleanup.** 目标候选人页保留公司资产 tab shell，不再退回独立“持续跟进”页面。目标候选人卡片的 headline、Public Web progress、公开主页链接 chips 已收敛为固定高度区域，长复核建议进入邻近 `?` 帮助说明，避免单个候选人卡片撑高整列。

## Highest Priority

### P0a: Model-native Search remains an explicit experiment, not a fallback

Status: guardrail added 2026-06-04. `model_native_search` is now a reserved search provider id with default-off settings and fail-closed provider-chain behavior. If it appears in `SEARCH_PROVIDER_ORDER`, `build_search_provider(...)` refuses to construct a fallback chain unless a future typed provider/command owner is implemented under `docs/MODEL_NATIVE_SEARCH_PROVIDER_CONTRACT.md`.

- [x] Document the contract: model-native Search may only collect supplemental evidence with owner/cost/provenance/retry/circuit/export/audit treatment; it must not silently replace DataForSEO, browser search, document fetch, or model adjudication.
- [x] Add fast preflight: `tests/test_search_provider.py::SearchProviderTest::test_model_native_search_provider_order_fails_closed_without_contract` is included in `make ci-pre-agent-contract`.
- [ ] Future optional experiment: after current Public Web W7g baseline is stable and model-provider credentials are healthy, run a reviewed A/B comparing DataForSEO-only vs DataForSEO plus model-native Search on fixed CRM record ids. Do not enable normal product use until independent review and live quality/cost/latency evidence pass.

### P0: Durable Execution Runtime convergence

Status: contract drafted in `docs/DURABLE_EXECUTION_RUNTIME_CONTRACT.md` on 2026-05-21. This is the next architecture convergence track. It should be treated as the control-plane foundation under the existing canonical projection / recovery / workflow phases, not as a separate optional refactor. The target is a durable execution runtime that can later serve acquisition workflows, profile refill, post-profile work, projection builders, CRM/Public Web/export, and Agent operations.

Implementation rule: do not expand `job_materialization_items` into the long-term command system. New normal-path execution should converge on append-only `workflow_events`, typed `workflow_commands`, reducer-owned state transitions, and command owner registry. `job_materialization_items` may be read by a report-visible migration adapter only, with an explicit retirement plan and gates that block new normal-path legacy writes.

  - Stage 1 progress payload must use one coherent semantic version. Lane counts, deduped count, and profile denominator cannot mix lifecycle rows, worker URL queues, and partial lane snapshots.
  - Profile scheduler slot-refill evaluator must distinguish true open-slot underuse from legal reserved/provider-owned/dispatch-claimed/deferred-by-lease states.
  - 2026-05-22 targeted evidence: OpenAI baseline+delta and Lovable live-roster long-latency rerun passed strict at `output/phase_w0_targeted_20260522_001152/summary.json` and `output/phase_w0_targeted_20260522_001152/report.json`. `board_runtime_state_parity.violation_case_count=0`, `profile_scheduler_contract.violation_detected=false`, and `progress_contract_violation_case_count=0`. The remaining broader matrix should run after W1/W2 foundation or before manual handoff, not as the first detector for basic contract drift.

Generic worker recovery now report-visible skips submitted `waiting_remote_harvest/search` workers with `run_id/dataset_id` until a durable `remote_provider_terminal_event` marker exists; terminal-event explicit wakeups remain recoverable. Targeted OpenAI/Lovable/Google rerun passed strict + Pre-Manual Signoff at `output/w6_targeted_20260523_134622/`: `board_runtime_state_parity.violation_case_count=0`, `progress_contract_violation_case_count=0`, all `23` provider invocations scripted, no duplicate dispatch signatures, no blocking/manual findings. Full W6 remains required for the full 5-case matrix and deletion signoff.

Reducers may write events/current-state/commands/outbox only; workers write events/results and do not generate arbitrary commands. 2026-05-22 evidence: `tests/test_durable_runtime.py` covers append-only/idempotent events, materialized current state, command idempotency/lease/terminal transition, runtime outbox idempotency, owner-registry ambiguity rejection, and deterministic reducer command/outbox output. `make ci-pg-contract` now bootstraps and writes the durable runtime tables under PG-only Testcontainers, including command claim -> running -> succeeded. This is foundation only; LinkedIn normal-path migration starts in W2.

Provider callbacks wake the runtime and record events only.
  - [x] **Phase W2a: reducer-owned writer and legacy write gate foundation.** Added `DurableRuntimeWriter`, which appends durable events, runs the pure reducer, and persists `workflow_current_state`, typed `workflow_commands`, and `runtime_outbox` through store APIs. Re-running the writer is idempotent and does not duplicate commands for an already planned idempotency key. The idempotency key includes job, snapshot, URL set, and submit scope so retry waves are not blocked by a prior normal-wave succeeded command.

Once a CRM writer ActivityAttempt, EntityDelta, or downstream command exists, cancellation fails closed and retry/resume must go through the command owner. 2026-05-26 status-surface follow-up: operation-run detail now returns `operation_run.status_summary`, list can opt in with `include_status_summary=true`, command detail returns a bounded `workflow_command.execution_summary`, command list can include the same summary with `include_execution_summary=true`, and operation-run provenance returns commands through the same API record shape; both summaries are read-only, fail-closed, and source status from operation/runtime tables rather than domain read models. Each query item uses stable `task_key` / `query_identity_key`, analogous to LinkedIn URL key, and downstream joins/retries/manifests/outcomes must use that key rather than provider response order. Provider response mapping must use provider-echoed `tag` or unambiguous provider-echoed `keyword`; missing provider identity fails closed to query-level retry/failure instead of request-order fallback. Successful query task ids stay submitted; only failed query items retry once through `retry_unit=search_query` / `retry_strategy=dataforseo_batch_failed_query_retry_only`. Concurrent `task_get` fetch also preserves sibling successes when one task id fails, recording failed fetch evidence as `retry_unit=dataforseo_task_id` / `retry_strategy=dataforseo_task_get_failed_task_retry_only`. Public Web batch submission now treats submit-failed checkpoints as item-level failed queries instead of keeping them as fake submitted work. `make ci-pre-agent-contract` includes this provider/Public Web regression slice so W6/Nightly is not the first detector for DataForSEO batch-envelope drift.

Domain tables such as `acquisition_discovery_lanes`, profile registry rows, CRM rows, Public Web rows, Excel rows, export rows, and projection rows remain owner-specific read models that reference command/activity causality; they must not become a second workflow engine. The intended Agent capability is to retry, cancel, resume, or inspect any typed activity such as provider poll, dataset fetch, parse, profile terminalization, Public Web signal materialization, export chunking, projection admission, index page build, or collection merge through Operation/Command/Activity APIs. W11/W10 preflight must block any new Agent-callable side effect that lacks command owner registry entry, control policy, Activity-spine policy, PG-only storage boundary, EntityDelta evidence, and a fail-closed domain read model contract.
  - 2026-05-26 W10 frontend contract follow-up: `contracts/frontend_api_contract.ts`, `contracts/frontend_api_contract.schema.json`, `contracts/frontend_api_adapter.ts`, and `docs/FRONTEND_API_CONTRACT.md` now include the Operation action registry, workflow command registry, command cancel/retry/resume responses, command/activity/attempt/entity-delta query responses, `display_contract`, `control_policy`, and `activity_spine_policy`. Command records and command control responses expose `display_contract`, `control_policy`, and `activity_spine_policy` at the top level as well as on `workflow_command`, keeping future Agent UI from inferring labels, command control, or Activity-spine requirements from command names. `operation_runtime.ActionRegistry` now blocks unregistered, duplicate, default-mismatched, and non-Agent-callable workflow command exposure at registration time; `ActionRegistry.to_record()` owns action-to-command contract enrichment directly, so non-HTTP Agent/runtime callers get the same owner/display/control/activity policy records as `/api/operations/action-registry`.

### P0: Canonical Serving Projection architecture

Status: contract drafted in `docs/CANONICAL_SERVING_PROJECTION_CONTRACT.md` on 2026-05-18. Implementation started on 2026-05-19 with the PG contract harness, ServingProjection storage/writer foundation, event-time run projection publication, collection-authoritative merge queue, projection public reader API v1, frontend `/projections/:projection_id` cutover, and the first profile-envelope/post-profile durable-unit decoupling slice.

CI-required mode fails when Testcontainers or Docker is unavailable; default local unittest mode skips unless explicitly enabled.

### P0: review1-review27 workflow contract closeout

These are newly reopened quality gates from the latest scripted-review/manual-browser loop. Older checked items in this file describe previous closure attempts; they do not supersede this section. Do not hand a scripted/browser run to manual testing while any item below remains open.

- [x] **Recovery/callback/daemon contract closeout.** Callback, profile-refill daemon, worker recovery, local-apply drain, board-visible drain, full-snapshot materialization, and housekeeping now have explicit ownership, phase ordering, phase elapsed metrics, and bounded synchronous work in code and docs. Remote provider event/job-scoped recovery disables search-seed discovery, full snapshot compaction, Excel recovery, and housekeeping by default. Smoke now preserves `recovery_phase_metrics` for every worker recovery run, exports `service_metrics.recovery_phase_metrics`, supports `require_no_recovery_phase_violation`, `max_recovery_phase_elapsed_ms`, and `max_recovery_total_elapsed_ms`, and Pre-Manual Signoff blocks missing/failed/slow/unexpected recovery phases including async `recovery_dispatch_count` evidence.
  - 2026-05-08 follow-up after Lovable `rerun45`: Stage 1 candidate documents now restore LinkedIn Stage 1 completion from `acquisition_stage.task_type='enrich_linkedin_profiles'` / `enrichment_scope='linkedin_stage_1'` only when profile-prefetch registry proof is terminal, so workflow resume does not rerun Stage 1 after all profile URLs are terminal and does not prematurely skip open Stage 1 documents. Job-scoped recovery defaults to same-job explicit resume instead of stale-only resume, preventing a quiescent job sidecar from leaving `workflow_open_count=1` for smoke-runner/manual takeover.
  - 2026-05-09 follow-up: remote-provider wakeup requests now union targeted worker ids across concurrent events and raise recovery `total_limit` only to the bounded remote-event burst limit. Hosted/acquiring workflow resume dispatches a bounded acquisition-resume owner thread instead of running full retrieval/finalization inline inside the recovery tick.
- [x] **Profile scheduler contract closeout.** `event_level_efficiency.profile_scheduler_contract` is now the canonical hard-gate report for same-wave ordinal dispatch, adaptive batch-size contract, retry-wave isolation, actor-slot refill, planned-dispatch owner identity, terminal queue leaks, and PG advisory-lock evidence. Smoke supports `require_no_profile_scheduler_contract_violation`; a 24/50 normal batch without explicit coalescing/tail/low-volume reason, mixed retry+normal wave, ownerless `planned_dispatch`, terminal queue leak, underfilled slot with deferred backlog, or required non-PG lock is blocking. The old `enrich(... full_roster_profile_prefetch=True)` direct planner/ThreadPool submit path is retired; worker-backed full-roster/scoped-search enrichment now enters the same `queue_background_profile_prefetch(...)` registry scheduler, and cache-hit completion hydrates only from registry/snapshot cache rather than synchronously calling the provider connector.
  - 2026-05-10 follow-up: `reserved_budget2` exposed a hard local handoff failure where terminal remote-provider owners still held `planned_dispatch` scheduler budget until local apply / board-visible work finished. Slot accounting now releases `planned_dispatch` occupancy once the owner worker has terminal status or `remote_provider_terminal_event_seen_at`, without moving registry scan/replan/submit into callbacks. Revalidation `output/profile_contract_pg_20260510_reserved_budget3/openai_agent_scoped_delta_streaming_report.json` passed with `expectation_failures=[]`, `local_to_next_submit_start_ms.max=0ms`, `remote_to_next_submit_start_ms.max=1243ms`, scheduler violation `false`, and Pre-Manual Signoff `passed` (`22/22` provider invocations scripted).
  - 2026-05-11 follow-up: large late shard is no longer unit-only coverage. `configs/scripted/openai_agent_large_late_shard_smoke_matrix.json` now runs a PG-backed no-baseline OpenAI scoped search where a 90-profile early shard is followed by a 600-profile late shard. Smoke expectations include `min_profile_batch_size_max` / `max_profile_batch_size_max`, so a late shard that falls back to 50-url batches or submits an unbounded oversized actor batch fails before manual testing. The 1600-URL late-shard formula remains covered by the focused scheduler unit test instead of the service smoke.
  - 2026-05-11 rerun4: the remaining large-late tail was not profile-slot underuse. Root cause was a post-profile prerequisite race: profile batches completed before search-seed shell rows were projected into `candidate_documents.json`, leaving `local_apply_closure` in `waiting_prerequisite` until the fallback timer. Incremental search-seed callbacks now project candidate documents before profile prefetch and record source-aware prerequisite reawaken metadata. PG-backed strict rerun `output/large_late_shard_pg_20260511_rerun4/report.json` passed with Pre-Manual Signoff clean, `provider_mode=scripted` for all `49` invocations, `stage_1_preview_to_final_results.max=18.61s` (down from the previous pressure finding around `53.8s`), `remote_to_next_submit_start_ms.max=1905ms`, `profile_scheduler_contract.violation_detected=false`, `post_profile_completion.slo_violation_detected=false`, `profile_file_visible_to_board_patch_visible.max=0ms`, and `all_profiles_fetched_to_all_cards_visible.max=0ms`.

Existing smoke SLO gates continue to enforce URL terminal leaks and visibility latency maxima.
  - 2026-05-11 follow-up: the main runner now uses the same post-profile barrier as recovery before promoting an existing terminal LinkedIn Stage 1 artifact. A terminal `candidate_documents.json` can complete `enrich_linkedin_profiles` only after `local_apply_closure` / post-profile daemon work is clear; open post-profile work keeps the workflow blocked instead of re-running provider work or advancing finalization. `local_apply_backlog` drains also split large same-scope closure groups by group/budget so recovery phases stay bounded and leave remaining durable items for later ticks.

Before enabling fallback burst, finalization must wait on complete post-profile visibility or durable full-snapshot materialization completion under pressure.

This closes the reporting gap exposed by the failed fallback-burst experiment; the remaining work is the actual finalization/barrier optimization, not report detection.

Full-current projection timing comes from `board_runtime_state.row_publication_updated_at` once the canonical board runtime state proves `delta_profile_board_visible_count >= target`. `stage_1_preview_to_final_results` now prefers backend stage-summary lag when available, while `job_to_final_results` remains the smoke-observed total wall-clock. Clean `recovery_tick_budget_exhausted` is no longer a warning either; it is `recovery_cooperative_budget_yield` when `next_tick_requested=true` and the same recovery report is otherwise clean. Remaining non-blocking warning class is dirty or insufficiently proven bounded recovery total elapsed / budget-yield optimization evidence when case SLOs still pass. Raw finalization lag still remains in reports for diagnostics, but provider wait is now excluded from `long_post_preview_finalization`, `long_finalization_after_preview`, and bottleneck decisions through `finalization_lag_evaluation_ms`; it should no longer appear as a known-acceptable warning by itself. - [ ] **Long-term Agent product architecture decoupling.** Current workflow orchestration is still too plan/run/job shaped for a sourcing copilot product. Future architecture work should separate local asset consumption from plan/run acquisition, so users can query, filter, search, edit, insert, delete, and enrich existing candidate/company assets without necessarily launching a new sourcing workflow. This should also cover external intake paths analogous to Excel intake, new acquisition lane definitions, multi-turn intent recognition, automatic execution of bounded user-requested operations, richer reuse of candidate/profile/evidence assets, and a clearer Agent runtime model. Evaluate whether LangGraph or another explicit state-machine/agent framework helps model long-running multi-step interactions, but keep storage, queue ownership, provider budgets, and serving projections deterministic and auditable.

- [ ] **Recovery fallback/dual-track observability gates.** Convert remaining recovery same-tick heavy phase and public-read compatibility fallback usage into smoke/signoff-visible metrics. Partial-overlay full rebuild fallback and finalization overlay full-rewrite fallback are now signoff-blocking when eligible (`partial_overlay_fast_path_fallback`, `partial_overlay_write_mode_report_missing`, `finalization_overlay_reuse_missed`, `finalization_overlay_candidate_count_drift`), so the remaining work is to keep reducing recovery same-tick continuation paths and to make any public-read compatibility fallback similarly report-visible. The intended steady state is not “fallback still passes”; it is “fallback is either absent under expected scripted cases or explicitly categorized as an optimization/compatibility finding.”

- [ ] **Recovery tick implementation complexity cleanup.** `run_worker_recovery_once` now has bounded phases and contract metrics, but it is still too large as a total scheduler and still contains multiple same-tick continuation decisions. After the overlay pressure fix and fallback gates, split the implementation further around durable phase owners so recovery ticks primarily record terminal evidence, release slots, enqueue work, and schedule bounded daemons.

- [ ] **Raw-profile acquired-asset search/filter contract.** Manual testing raised a valid product need to search acquired raw profile content directly (for example terms like `Gemini` / `RL`) instead of relying only on materialized card fields or starting a new job. This is intentionally deferred from the current board-runtime sync cleanup because it needs an asset/manifest/index contract: searchable raw-profile scope, freshness watermark, query/filter API, pagination cost budget, and whether counts are global, current-served partial, raw-profile partial, or unavailable. Do not bolt raw-profile scanning into the frontend filter path until that serving/index contract exists.

### P0: manual-browser contract findings still requiring closure

These items came from the 2026-05-07 manual OpenAI/Lovable browser investigation after the earlier PG scripted closeout. Treat them as open until the implementation and regression gates below are complete. Do not run another PG-backed browser/smoke confidence pass as a final sign-off if any item here remains open.

- [x] **Scripted provider matcher source purity.** `payload_contains` / `payload_excludes` now match only the serialized provider request payload, never `runtime_dir`, `request_context`, scenario name, or other environment strings. Full-context matching remains explicit through `context_contains` / `context_excludes`. Regression `tests/test_scripted_provider_scenario.py::test_scripted_provider_payload_terms_ignore_runtime_context` proves a Lovable request under a runtime path containing `openai_agent_delta_streaming` cannot match an OpenAI Agent rule, and `lovable_live_roster.json` now carries explicit Lovable payload scope for company roster and former profile search rules.
- [x] **Final board-runtime counters must outrank stale partial patches.** Once the served projection is full `current_snapshot_serving` and final `asset_population.card_materialization_summary` proves the full population/card counts, `board_runtime_state` must not let an older partial patch summary freeze public counters. Regression `tests/test_results_api.py::ResultsApiTest::test_board_runtime_state_final_serving_counters_outrank_stale_partial_patch` covers the observed shape: final asset summary `597/597/297`, latest partial patch `548/597` and `248/297`; board runtime now reports final serving counts while retaining patch sequence as watermark context.
- [x] **Run-status and candidate-board progress wording must share one canonical event-time projection.** Stage 1 lane metrics may remain a separate execution detail line, but user-facing profile fetch/card materialization wording must not be derived once from `linkedin_stage_1_progress` on the execution page and again from `board_runtime_state` on the candidate board with different denominators or counts. Regression `tests/test_frontend_candidate_sync_summary.py::FrontendCandidateSyncSummaryTest::test_board_runtime_summary_ignores_conflicting_lifecycle_and_stage1_progress` locks the frontend rule: when `boardRuntimeState` exists, lifecycle/stage1 counters are compatibility/diagnostic inputs only and cannot override board runtime status text.
- [x] **Patch freshness must have one monotonic ordering rule.** `board_runtime_state.row_publication_tier` is now the canonical tier: `current_snapshot_serving` outranks `partial_patch`; `row_publication_sequence` orders only within the same tier. Backend exposes the tier, shared contract types/schema include it, and frontend merge logic no longer lets a higher-sequence stale partial patch regress a verified final/current serving projection. Regression coverage: `tests/test_results_api.py::ResultsApiTest::test_board_runtime_state_final_serving_counters_outrank_stale_partial_patch` asserts final tier, and `tests/test_frontend_candidate_filters.py::FrontendCandidateFiltersTest::test_board_runtime_final_projection_outranks_newer_partial_sequence` locks the frontend merge rule.
- [x] **Lovable empty-board streaming regression.** After matcher pollution is fixed, live-roster shell-row deferral, local apply, and board-visible patch publication are covered by service gates that distinguish pure shell rows from display-ready/needs-completion/low-richness consumable cards. `require_no_placeholder_to_final_board_jump`, `min_board_visible_patch_consumable_card_count`, and `min_board_visible_consumable_card_progression_count` are enabled in Lovable/live-roster and baseline+delta matrices, so placeholder-to-final jumps fail before manual browser sign-off.
- [x] **Post-profile completion contract implementation/gates.** `docs/EVENT_LEVEL_WORKFLOW_RESPONSE.md` defines the full post-profile state machine: provider terminal evidence -> URL item terminal states -> next-submit opportunity -> deterministic `local_apply_closure` -> bounded event-level drain -> `board_visible_delta_apply` patch -> background `snapshot_full_materialization` -> serving finalization -> post-result layering. The event-level drain is now budgeted across its two substeps: if local apply claims work and exceeds `EVENT_LEVEL_BOARD_VISIBLE_AFTER_LOCAL_APPLY_BUDGET_MS`, board-visible apply stays in the durable queue for the next bounded phase instead of extending the same recovery tick. Smoke now exposes `service_metrics.post_profile_completion` and hard gates URL terminal-state recording, `profile_file_visible -> board_patch_visible`, `all_profiles_fetched -> all_cards_visible`, and bounded event-level callback elapsed time through `max_post_profile_url_terminal_state_leak_count`, `max_profile_file_visible_to_board_patch_visible_ms`, `max_all_profiles_fetched_to_all_cards_visible_ms`, and `max_event_level_materialization_callback_elapsed_ms`.
- [x] **Pre-Manual Scripted Signoff Review.** Scripted smoke success alone is not sufficient before user manual browser testing. `scripts/review_scripted_smoke_run.py` now reads the per-case smoke report plus optional summary and emits fixed JSON/Markdown signoff sections: `passed_gates`, `manual_review_required_findings`, `known_acceptable_warnings`, and `blocking_findings`. Blocking findings include not-ready smoke cases, expectation failures, provider invocations outside expected no-cost mode, cross-endpoint board-runtime drift, post-profile SLO violations, and non-replayable board-visible projection evidence. Any blocking finding means the run must not be handed to manual testing.
- [x] **Test control plane should become PG-only for workflow simulation.** SQLite remains useful for isolated unit tests, but scripted/browser workflow confidence now uses the same PG queue/lock semantics as production. `isolated_hosted_test_runtime`, `run_simulate_smoke_matrix.py --runtime-dir`, `run_explain_dry_run_matrix.py --runtime-dir`, `seed_reference_smoke_runtime.py`, and `dev_scripted_openai_agent_delta.sh` require a runtime-scoped PG-only env (`postgres_only + shared_memory`) and prepare safe `sourcing_scripted*`/`sourcing_simulate*`/`sourcing_replay*`/`sourcing_test*` schemas before backend startup. Missing PG now fails closed instead of falling back to SQLite. Follow-up: migrate any remaining older hosted smoke tests that hand-roll stores to the shared runtime helper, and eventually remove disk SQLite compatibility from workflow-like tests.

### Production-final convergence / fallback retirement register

This register tracks recent stabilization work that reduced live risk but is not yet the production-final shape. Treat every item here as multi-path pollution risk until its retirement gate is complete. Do not add new fallback ladders on top of these paths; either promote the durable owner or delete/narrow the compatibility path.

Suggested closure order: lifecycle backfill/helper deletion -> local apply ownership -> board-visible/full-materialization mirror retirement -> profile queue-first scheduler -> discovery/provider retry item ownership -> authoritative ECS migration/repair -> Excel/public-read repair closure -> browser/scripted service gates.

Retry creates a fresh force-refresh run/worker for `failed`, `cancelled`, `completed_with_errors`, or `needs_review`, preserving the old run as audit history rather than reopening a polluted checkpoint. Authoritative candidate detail now returns the materialized shard record, and single/batch detail overlays rank/confidence metadata without letting stale `job_results` or SQLite candidate fields overwrite shard-owned profile data. `/results?include_candidates=1` and public ranked-results payloads also read materialized/summary rows without raw timeline hydration. Frontend board pagination and the bare `/api/jobs/{job_id}/candidates` endpoint default to `lightweight=1`. Single-candidate public detail and internal `get_job_candidate_detail(...)` default to no raw timeline hydration; only explicit diagnostic opt-in `hydrate_legacy_timeline=1` may enter the old resolver. Batch detail remains materialized-only. Job-scoped baseline+delta overlays are now treated as serving projections, not profile-field truth: sparse overlay rows are enriched from current/baseline materialized serving shards before public serialization. Public reads also no longer auto-materialize or fallback to raw `candidate_documents.json`; legacy candidate-doc materialization requires explicit opt-in/repair/backfill, and completed-workflow repair now materializes serving artifacts before publishing a source path. Remaining risk: raw LinkedIn timeline resolver still exists for legacy CLI/internal export/profile-completion/backfill and target-candidate export packaging paths; do not add new normal public-read dependencies on it.

### Service-grade streaming workflow rebuild

- 2026-05-02 planning prerequisite landed: `AUTHORITATIVE_ASSET_COVERAGE_CONTRACT.md` now defines authoritative serving pointer vs full-company coverage proof vs exact scoped shard coverage. `asset_reuse_planning` and `organization_execution_profile` both read `build_population_coverage_contract(...)`, so scoped-only authoritative assets no longer silently unlock full-company reuse. Keep this workstream before queue-first scheduler and partial board streaming because a wrong `reuse_snapshot_only` decision prevents downstream streaming work from starting at all.
- 2026-05-02 intent priority prerequisite landed: `INTENT_STRATEGY_SOURCE_PRIORITY_CONTRACT.md` defines `requested_population_boundary` and the priority order `user boundary > coverage proof > authoritative pointer > profile default`. Directional queries with all-members wording now use full-company local filtering only when full-company coverage is proven; otherwise they remain scoped directional. `execution_semantics` now requires full-company proof before showing "full local asset reuse".
- 2026-05-02 read-only audit prerequisite landed: `audit-authoritative-reuse-planning` reports `requested_population_boundary`, authoritative pointer, selected planning row, full-company coverage proof, scoped shard rows, asset reuse planner outcome, execution semantics, and warnings without warming profiles or rebuilding ledgers.
- 2026-05-02 planner parity matrix landed: `configs/planner_parity/authoritative_reuse_planning_matrix.json`, `audit-authoritative-reuse-planning-matrix`, and `compare-authoritative-reuse-planning-matrix` now provide a read-only ECS/local report. Local run saved `runtime/audits/local-authoritative-reuse-planning.json` with `status=ok`, `case_count=9`, `failure_count=0`; it also surfaced the OpenAI Health issue as `baseline_generation_lags_same_snapshot_shard_materialization` rather than query parsing or missing shard registry.
- 2026-05-02 coverage backfill CLI landed: `backfill-authoritative-population-coverage` converts already-proven registry coverage into explicit `population_coverage` metadata. It defaults to dry-run and does not repair materialization generations. Local OpenAI/Meta dry-run reports `changed_count=2`, `persisted_count=0`.
- 2026-05-02 local serving-generation repair landed: `repair-authoritative-serving-generation` is an explicit offline repair/republication command for cases where selected shard rows exist but the authoritative serving generation does not subsume same-snapshot shard materializations. It defaults to dry-run, calls no providers, creates a new repair snapshot on `--apply`, rebuilds normalized artifacts, preserves selected source snapshot provenance, and reports scope mismatches as data-quality warnings instead of planner repair failures. Local OpenAI Health was repaired to `20260501T222111`; a fresh dry-run now returns `no_repair_needed` with `reuse_snapshot_only`.
- 2026-05-02 selected source provenance normalization landed: `normalize-authoritative-source-provenance` keeps `selected_snapshot_ids` to the serving snapshot plus source snapshots with reusable `acquisition_shard_registry` proof, archiving old no-shard ids into metadata. Local OpenAI dropped 4 old no-shard selected ids; Meta dropped 1. The local planner parity matrix now reports `selected_snapshot_missing_shard_registry_row_count=0`, `baseline_generation_lags_same_snapshot_shard_materialization=false`, and no warnings across all 9 default cases.
- 2026-05-03 publication invariant landed locally: authoritative registration now runs shard registry/bundle refresh before pointer promotion, validates same-snapshot shard subsumption against the candidate serving generation, and republishes a no-provider repair snapshot before publishing if the generation lags selected shard materializations. The offline repair CLI remains for historical/ECS rows, not for normal publication correctness.

Use `docs/CLAUDE_CODE_STREAMING_WORKFLOW_REBUILD_CONTEXT.md` and `docs/STREAMING_WORKFLOW_REBUILD_PLAN.md` as the next Claude Code handoff before changing orchestration code again.
- First slice design landed 2026-04-30: see `docs/JOB_RESULT_LIFECYCLE_DESIGN.md` for the canonical persisted `job_result_lifecycle` schema, writer/reader ownership, and migration plan. Failing anchor regression `tests/test_results_api.py::ResultsApiTest::test_lifecycle_consistent_across_dashboard_progress_and_candidate_page_for_openai_infra_shape` reproduces job `c5248ea4b3b4` and asserts /dashboard, /progress, /candidates observe the same lifecycle row. Currently fails with /dashboard `served_candidate_count=5` vs /progress `1200` — exact evidence of the per-request rebuild divergence. Implementing the canonical row must make this test pass.

The Infra cross-API regression now passes. Remaining work in this slice: (a) move writes from "every public read refreshes the row" to dedicated workflow events (workflow create / baseline publication / Stage 1 projection / materialization-repoint); (b) populate Stage 1 lane projection columns; (c) one-shot backfill for existing jobs.

Four writer tests added; the two pre-existing direct-builder failures triaged and updated to the new contract. Worker URL queue size is exposed via `metadata_json.workers.queued_profile_url_count` only, never as the public denominator — the OpenAI Infra mixed-source bug is impossible by construction. Four new Stage 1 writer tests; full lifecycle suite at 30 passed. - Slice 4 implementation landed 2026-04-30: moved `update_job_result_lifecycle_from_stage1` from public-read seams to the real workflow event-time hook (`_persist_running_job_inline_reconcile_state`). The Stage 1 writer now runs when `acquisition_progress.latest_state` is updated with a new snapshot after search-seed/roster workers complete, immediately after `save_job`. Public reads (`/dashboard`, `/progress`, `/candidates`) reverted from `_compute_and_record_stage1_progress` back to the non-mutating `_build_linkedin_stage1_progress_payload` — they no longer write to the canonical lifecycle row for Stage 1 fields. `_compute_and_record_stage1_progress` deprecated; `_lifecycle_row_needs_stage1_repair` narrowed to pure legacy/backfill (no `projection_source_snapshot_id` AND no Stage 1 counters). One new test (`test_job_result_lifecycle_stage1_event_time_write_before_public_read`) proves public reads don't mutate. Full lifecycle suite at 31 passed. Remaining gaps: (a) materialization-repoint wiring; (b) failed-state wiring; (c) one-shot legacy backfill to delete `_lifecycle_row_needs_stage1_repair` and `_compute_and_record_stage1_progress` outright; (d) retire `_build_result_view_lifecycle_payload` from public-read flow once (a) ships.

The materialization writer updates the canonical lifecycle row with `served_snapshot_id`, `served_candidate_count`, and `current_snapshot_id` before any public read can observe the new result view. One new test (`test_job_result_lifecycle_baseline_delta_overlay_for_health_whisper_shape`) proves the writer records the overlay serving state for the Health/Whisper shape; the test documents the current gap (canonical reader's fast path requires a stored result_view) with commented-out TODO assertions. Baseline+delta jobs cannot complete into raw delta-only result views at the event-time level, but the canonical reader's fallback projection can still rebuild from old sources when no stored result_view exists. - Slice 6 implementation landed 2026-04-30: retired `_build_result_view_lifecycle_payload` from the canonical reader fast path. Public reads (`/dashboard`, `/progress`, `/candidates`) now trust the validated `job_result_lifecycle` row as the source of truth, even when no stored `result_view` exists yet. Fixed `_lifecycle_row_needs_stage1_repair` to not require Stage 1 counters when the row has `served_snapshot_id` (materialization writer is authoritative). Quarantined `_build_result_view_lifecycle_payload` for legacy/backfill only: added `_allow_for_legacy_backfill` parameter that raises `ValueError` if called for validated rows without explicit flag. Enabled full cross-API consistency assertions in `test_job_result_lifecycle_baseline_delta_overlay_for_health_whisper_shape`. Added three new regression tests proving validated row without result_view uses canonical row, terminal invariant enforcement, and fallback projection quarantine. Full lifecycle suite at 22 passed. The Health/Whisper baseline+delta overlay regression (where `/dashboard`, `/progress`, `/candidates` returned divergent lifecycle payloads) is now impossible because all three APIs read from the same canonical row, and the fallback projection is quarantined for legacy/backfill only. Remaining gaps: (a) failed-state wiring; (b) one-shot legacy backfill.
