All three call sites now write `phase="failed"`, `state="failed"`, `phase_status="terminal"` to the canonical lifecycle row before returning failure. Fixed critical bugs: (1) `_normalize_lifecycle_for_terminal_phase` was overwriting `state="failed"` with materialization state; (2) `_lifecycle_row_needs_stage1_repair` was triggering repair for terminal rows; (3) cache block indentation bug causing `UnboundLocalError`; (4) `allow_materialization_fallback` logic inversion; (5) Stage 1 repair detection field name errors; (6) state normalization not checking job stage. Added comprehensive failed-state test coverage: `test_job_result_lifecycle_failed_workflow_writes_terminal_row`, `test_job_result_lifecycle_excel_intake_failure_writes_terminal_row`, `test_job_result_lifecycle_retrieval_job_failure_writes_terminal_row`, `test_job_result_lifecycle_failed_state_consistent_across_endpoints`, `test_job_result_lifecycle_failed_public_read_does_not_mutate`. Full test suite at 98 passed. Failed workflows cannot appear as "unavailable" or other incorrect states because the terminal failure writer commits the canonical failed state before any public read. Cross-endpoint consistency guaranteed by all three APIs (`/dashboard`, `/progress`, `/candidates`) reading from the same canonical row. Remaining gaps: (a) one-shot legacy backfill.
- Slice 8 implementation landed 2026-05-02: implemented `backfill_job_result_lifecycle` in `src/sourcing_agent/job_result_lifecycle_backfill.py` to create lifecycle rows for historical jobs without validated rows. Backfill scans all jobs, skips those with `source_validation_status='validated'`, builds projections using quarantined `_build_result_view_lifecycle_payload` with `_allow_for_legacy_backfill=True`, and persists via `_persist_canonical_lifecycle` with `source_validation_status='validated'` so backfilled rows are trusted by fast path reader. CLI command `backfill-job-result-lifecycle` added with `--dry-run` flag; uses `build_orchestrator()` for correct initialization. Idempotent: re-running backfill on jobs with validated rows is safe and skips them. 2026-05-02 hardening after real CLI dry-run: schema preflight now force-syncs/validates the single `job_result_lifecycle` table into PG before the first lifecycle read, fixing the prior `UndefinedTable` production-path failure; CLI verbose progress callback signature is tested; `get_job_candidate_page` no longer calls the deprecated `_compute_and_record_stage1_progress` wrapper. Local real dry-run now completes with `249` jobs pending backfill and `0` errors. Legacy helpers (`_build_result_view_lifecycle_payload`, `_lifecycle_row_needs_stage1_repair`, `_compute_and_record_stage1_progress`) retained as safety fallback until production backfill runs. Next steps: run `backfill-job-result-lifecycle --dry-run` in production/ECS to preview changes, execute production backfill, then delete the three legacy helper methods outright. Validation caveat: one full `tests/test_results_api.py` run had a teardown-only temp directory cleanup failure; the failing test passed when rerun alone and should be tracked as cleanup flakiness before final release validation.
- Why this is now first:
  - Repeated failures came from the same architectural gap: result lifecycle, progress counters, scheduler handoff, materialization, and public serving are still derived from multiple non-atomic sources.
  - Scripted/browser tests improved, but they still did not force enough negative assertions for stale result views, mixed-source progress, actor-slot underuse, and public API slow paths before hosted/live usage.
  - More frontend wording fixes or one-off read repairs will keep hiding symptoms unless the backend has one canonical lifecycle writer and atomic progress projection.
- Required first implementation slice:
  - [ ] Add or converge on a persisted `job_result_lifecycle` record keyed by job/result view, owning baseline/current/served snapshot ids, expected/served counts, delta fetched/applied/materialized/board-visible counts, phase, and transition timestamps.
  - [ ] Route `/progress`, `/dashboard`, `/candidates`, frontend history, workflow completion, result-view publication, materialization, and read repair through this lifecycle path instead of re-deriving state from `job.summary`, `job_result_views.metadata`, `candidate_source`, and stage files.
  - [ ] Make LinkedIn Stage 1 progress atomic: current/former/all lane counts, dedupe, profile denominator, fetched, applied, and materialized counters must come from one projection snapshot or expose worker-derived queued URL state as a separate technical metric.
  - [ ] Split provider completion from heavy local work: local apply and next-submit opportunity must happen before artifact rebuild, retrieval-index rebuild, facet generation, outreach layering, or long cache-marker confirmation.
  - [ ] Replace group-drain batch processing with queue-first scheduling: durable discovery/profile/apply/materialization items are the retry/dedupe/progress units, while provider batches are only adaptive remote-run envelopes.
  - [ ] Add an adaptive batch packer with target size, minimum non-tail size, coalescing window, oldest-item max wait, queue-quiescent final-tail flush, retry-isolation flush, low-volume company mode, and explicit small-batch reason.
    - 2026-05-02 service baseline landed: Harvest profile prefetch now returns per-run `batch_envelopes` with batch size, deferred backlog, actor/submit budgets, dispatch strategy, `small_batch_reason`, `tiny_batch_allowed`, and provider-slot-underuse fields. `workflow_efficiency` aggregates these into scripted/browser event-level guardrails and fails on unexplained tiny live batches or idle actor slots while backlog remains. The same slice also coalesces tiny chunks within a single ready dispatch wave before provider submit and reports `tiny_batch_coalesced_count`. This is not the final queue-first scheduler replacement because it does not yet persist item-level scheduling checkpoints or coalesce across future/near-ready arrivals.
    - 2026-05-02 queue contract slice landed: Harvest profile prefetch now returns `profile_prefetch_queue` with explicit `linkedin_profile_registry` item-store semantics: requested/cached/ready/newly-queued/already-queued/deferred/failed/pending counts, queue-quiescence flags, oldest pending item age, slot occupancy basis, and registry status counts. `workflow_efficiency` aggregates these watermarks so scripted/browser reports can reason about queue state without scraping URL lists or provider logs. This remains a contract/observability slice; the final scheduler still needs durable item-level scheduling checkpoints and cross-event coalescing.
    - 2026-05-02 materialization observability landed: running inline materialization now emits structured `workflow_materialization` job events (`materialize_deferred`, `materialize_started`, `materialize_completed|failed|unknown`) and `workflow_smoke` / `workflow_efficiency` count them together with `completed_workflow_reconcile`. Provider case reports also roll up `sync_result.materialization_streaming` from those events, so `materialization_streaming.report_available` no longer depends on job summary side effects. The strict OpenAI Agent scoped-delta scripted smoke now passes with `materialization_streaming.report_available=true`, `post_preview_finalization.materialize_completed_count=1`, `event_level_efficiency.violation_detected=false`, profile batch size range `15..25`, no unexplained tiny batches, no provider-slot-underuse-with-backlog, and `final_results_to_board_nonempty_ms≈513ms`. This closes the "materialization completed but timeline/report cannot observe it" failure, but it does not yet implement row-level partial delta board streaming.
    - 2026-05-02 packer extraction slice landed: `ProfilePrefetchBatchPlan` now centralizes ready URL normalization, dispatch-window selection, in-wave tiny coalescing, worker-budget split, deferred URL accounting, and plan reason for both `queue_background_profile_prefetch` and `enrich(..., full_roster_profile_prefetch=True)`. Profile URLs are now `ProfilePrefetchQueueItem` records backed by `linkedin_profile_registry`, and `workflow_efficiency` aggregates compact plan counts (`queue_item_count`, `planned_dispatch_item_count`, `planned_deferred_item_count`). The plan also emits continuous-refill audit fields (`available_slot_count`, `planned_new_worker_count`, `unfilled_available_slot_count`, `underfilled_with_deferred_items`, `refill_saturation`). Next bounded step: explicit refill entrypoint/loop trigger over this item plan; do not duplicate the same tiny-tail/backpressure rules in another call site.
    - 2026-05-02 explicit refill trigger slice landed: Harvest profile completion now records `profile_refill_trigger` before materialization while preserving the compatible `_queue_background_profile_prefetch_after_harvest_ingest` helper. The trigger records provider-completion source/reason, `linkedin_profile_registry` item store, elapsed time, dispatch/deferred counts, and the batch-plan slot-fill fields. `workflow_efficiency` aggregates `profile_prefetch_refill` so service reports can distinguish "provider completion opened a refill opportunity and filled slots" from "later recovery happened to submit another batch." Remaining gap: this is an event envelope around the existing planner, not yet a durable item scheduler/daemon.
    - 2026-05-02 durable refill item-state slice landed: `linkedin_profile_registry` now carries scheduler state (`refill_queue_state`, last refill trigger/plan/deferred reason, planned timestamp, attempt count) in both SQLite and PG, with a PG refill-queue index. `queue_background_profile_prefetch` records durable URL item state after `ProfilePrefetchBatchPlan`, and queue/efficiency reports aggregate `refill_queue_state_counts`. This keeps lifecycle status separate from scheduler state; budget-deferred URLs are no longer indistinguishable from active queued remote work. Superseded detail: active URLs are no longer written directly as provider-owned `planned_dispatch` at plan time; the 2026-05-03 dispatch-ownership slice below inserted `dispatch_claimed`.
    - 2026-05-02 registry-backed refill selector slice landed: `list_linkedin_profile_refill_queue_items` reads durable `deferred_budget` URL items by job/snapshot, and `queue_background_profile_prefetch` folds them back into the same `ProfilePrefetchBatchPlan`. A completion event can now refill from registry-only deferred items even when the current event has no new candidate URLs.
    - 2026-05-02 service-loop refill slice landed: `run_worker_recovery_once` now runs `_run_profile_prefetch_refill_queue_once` after worker recovery/reconcile. The selector groups `linkedin_profile_registry.refill_queue_state=deferred_budget` items by `source_job + snapshot_dir` via `list_linkedin_profile_refill_queue_groups`, invokes the same `queue_background_profile_prefetch`/`ProfilePrefetchBatchPlan` path with no candidate-artifact scan, and records `profile_prefetch_refill` in the daemon result. `WorkerDaemonService` treats refill dispatch as activity, so the service loop immediately continues when registry items were submitted instead of sleeping after slot availability. Remaining gap: this is still profile-URL queue-first only; discovery rows, local apply deltas, and board-visible materialization patches are not yet durable item queues.
    - 2026-05-03 profile retry ownership slice landed: retryable profile fetch failures now write `refill_queue_state='retry_wait'` only when the registry row has workflow recovery scope (`source_jobs` and `last_snapshot_dir`). Workflow-triggered snapshot profile completion passes `source_job_id` into the registry; maintenance/offline failures remain `failed_retryable` lifecycle rows but do not become daemon-drainable orphan retry items. The service-loop selector scans `deferred_budget`, `deferred_coalescing`, and `retry_wait` through the same grouped `source_job + snapshot_dir` path.
    - 2026-05-03 dispatch ownership slice landed as an intermediate state: active URLs selected by `ProfilePrefetchBatchPlan` were first written as `dispatch_claimed` with a short `refill_not_before_at`, so the submit window was durable but not mistaken for provider ownership. This preserved the provider-owned distinction but still made plan-time selected rows look claimed too early. The 2026-05-07 replan-window contract below supersedes that part: plan/replan records only unsubmitted deferred backlog, and `dispatch_claimed` is written only after provider limiter/slot confirmation.
    - 2026-05-07 replan-window contract slice landed: profile prefetch replan now operates only over unsubmitted scheduler-owned normal URL items in `linkedin_profile_registry`, and `8` is the target envelope count for the current replannable set rather than a workflow-wide cap. Append-trigger replan reads `deferred_coalescing` with `ready_only=false` so 25+25 probe arrivals can form one normal envelope before the timer, while daemon/timer refill still respects `refill_not_before_at`. Replan no longer writes active URLs as `dispatch_claimed`; dispatch claim is written only after provider limiter/slot confirmation and immediately before worker/provider submit. PG deployments serialize the short replan/submit-claim critical sections with transaction-scoped try-advisory lock; a busy lock yields `profile_prefetch_scheduler_lock_busy` instead of blocking the recovery tick. SQLite fallback remains process-local test compatibility only.
    - 2026-05-08 same-wave reservation fix landed: active chunks selected by a plan are now written as short-TTL `dispatch_reserved` under the per `source_job + snapshot_dir` scheduler lock. This closes the rerun51 failure mode where app/recovery callbacks both planned the same 115-URL wave and fragmented it into slow `50/44/21` tails. Append-trigger replan still ignores `deferred_coalescing.not_before_at` for probe coalescing, but it must respect `dispatch_reserved` / `dispatch_claimed` timers; provider-slot or worker-begin backpressure returns reserved rows to `deferred_budget` with the concrete reason, slot-confirmed submit promotes to `dispatch_claimed`, and remote ownership promotes to `planned_dispatch`.
  - [ ] Make tiny live profile batches such as `2` or `3` auto-merge into ready/near-ready work by default. They should be impossible unless queue quiescence is proven after the coalescing window, or retry-isolation/low-volume-company policy records why merging would be worse.
    - 2026-05-02 strict-smoke hardening fixed two more edge cases: provider/limiter `backpressure` envelopes are exempt from idle actor-slot-underuse violations, and unproven tiny final-tail batches are deferred while sibling profile workers remain active instead of submitting a single-profile provider run.
    - 2026-05-02 coalescing-window guard landed: for non-low-volume companies, a fresh high-volume `1`-`5` URL tail is marked `deferred_coalescing` in `linkedin_profile_registry` and released instead of submitting a live provider run immediately. A later attempt can flush it as `queue_quiescent_final_tail` only after `HARVEST_PROFILE_TINY_TAIL_COALESCING_MIN_AGE_MS` elapses. Remaining gap: this still relies on future event/recovery attempts rather than a dedicated durable queue scheduler with timers.
    - 2026-05-03 final timer-owner slice landed: fresh high-volume tiny tails no longer create fake `harvest_profile_batch` timer workers. The coalescing deadline is stored on `linkedin_profile_registry.refill_not_before_at` with `refill_queue_state='deferred_coalescing'`, and the service-loop refill selector wakes ready items through the same `ProfilePrefetchBatchPlan` path. `waiting_profile_coalescing` is legacy pollution only: recoverable-worker scans exclude it, and efficiency metrics count it as a violation if it appears.
  - [ ] Continuously refill provider slots from ready profile URL items while budget remains; same-kind worker drain may gate heavy full materialization but must not gate next-submit or lightweight board-visible apply.
    - 2026-05-02 transition state: provider completion now emits `profile_refill_trigger` with batch-plan slot metrics, so scripted/browser reports can observe refill opportunities. Next implementation should persist refillable URL items / attempts as durable queue state and let a scheduler drain ready items across completion events without re-deriving URL lists in orchestrator.
    - 2026-05-02 item-state transition: refillable profile URL items are now persisted in `linkedin_profile_registry`, so the next scheduler loop should read that durable state instead of rebuilding deferred URL lists from result summaries or candidate artifacts.
    - 2026-05-02 selector transition: workflow-triggered refill reads the durable registry queue, and the worker recovery service now invokes the same selector on every tick. Next step is to move beyond profile URLs: create durable apply/materialization queue items so board-visible patches can stream independently of full snapshot compaction.
  - [ ] Add partial delta board streaming through a continuous serving projection: baseline generation + ordered delta patches + row-level materialization state. Full current snapshot/retrieval/index becomes background compaction, not the first moment new candidates can be visible.
    - 2026-05-02 first board-visible projection slice landed: completed Harvest profile batches can now publish a job-scoped baseline+delta overlay while same-kind workers are still in flight. The materializer first writes the completed delta candidates into the control plane, then `_publish_partial_board_visible_delta_overlay` writes a real serving projection, upserts `job_result_view`, and updates `job_result_lifecycle.delta_profile_board_visible_count`. The lifecycle writer only repoints `served_snapshot_id` when a `serving_projection_id` exists, so board-visible progress cannot be a count-only fiction. Follow-up profile batches for the same current snapshot accumulate into the existing partial projection instead of getting blocked after the first partial repoint.
    - 2026-05-02 durable patch-log slice landed: `job_board_visible_patches` is now the ordered, idempotent replay source for board-visible delta ids. Result-view/lifecycle metadata remains a mirror, and the follow-up-batch regression now proves accumulation survives metadata loss. Remaining gap: discovery rows, local apply deltas, provider retry attempts, and full materialization/compaction still need unified durable item queues.
    - 2026-05-02 service-metrics guardrail landed: provider/smoke reports now include `service_metrics.board_visible_projection`, derived from lifecycle plus durable patch rows or `workflow_materialization.board_visible_delta_applied` events. It flags projection-missing visible counts, patch-log missing/replay lag, metadata replay dependency, and fetched-to-board-visible lag. Next step is to use these metrics in scripted/browser hard gates while implementing unified queue-first apply/materialization items.
    - 2026-05-02 board-visible apply item queue landed: `job_materialization_items(item_kind='board_visible_delta_apply')` now stores pending/running/retryable/completed overlay apply work. `_inline_incremental_sync_for_running_job` enqueues the item immediately after `synchronize_snapshot_candidate_delta`, attempts inline claim/process for low latency, and leaves failures retryable. `run_worker_recovery_once` now drains this queue, so a transient overlay writer failure or process death no longer requires another Harvest webhook before the candidate board catches up. Remaining gap: full snapshot compaction/materialization and discovery/local-apply deltas still need the same item-queue treatment.
    - 2026-05-02 snapshot-full materialization item queue landed: `job_materialization_items(item_kind='snapshot_full_materialization')` now stores pending/running/retryable/completed full normalized artifacts/retrieval/index compaction work. Workflow completion and background reconcile scheduling enqueue the item before the compatibility background thread runs; `run_worker_recovery_once` drains it through claim/lease/retry semantics, so a service tick can recover scheduled full compaction without another webhook. Remaining gap: discovery rows, local snapshot-apply deltas, provider retry attempts, and cross-event adaptive batching still need the same item-queue treatment.
    - 2026-05-02 historical snapshot-full backfill landed: `backfill-snapshot-full-materialization-items` defaults to dry-run and scans completed workflow summaries with `background_snapshot_materialization.status=scheduled|deferred`, then enqueues deterministic `snapshot_full_materialization` items with `--apply`. This is the migration/backlog sweep counterpart to the bounded service scan.
    - 2026-05-03 snapshot-full queue observability landed: `GET /api/jobs/{job_id}/materialization-items` exposes read-only item diagnostics, and `workflow_smoke` feeds those rows into `service_metrics.snapshot_full_materialization_queue`. Scripted/hosted summary rollups now report backlog, retryable, and stale-running case counts so full compaction drift is visible even when summary mirrors are stale.
    - 2026-05-03 local apply backlog observability landed: `service_metrics.local_apply_backlog` now reports workers that wrote `output.inline_incremental_apply` but have not written the matching `output.inline_incremental_ingest` gate. It exposes applied-not-ingested count, stale count, age summary, worker/recovery kind breakdowns, samples, bottleneck entries, and smoke summary rollups. This is the acceptance baseline for a future durable local-apply queue; it is not a second queue and should not be expanded into one without reconciling the existing worker marker contract with `job_materialization_items`.
    - 2026-05-03 local apply backlog callback drain landed: `run_worker_recovery_once` now drains completed apply-only workers through the existing completion callback path before profile-refill/materialization queues. It only resumes local Phase B/C closure from stored worker output and snapshot state; it does not re-execute provider actors or re-run Phase A apply. `WorkerDaemonService` treats the drain as activity and reports cumulative claimed/completed counts. This is a bridge for current worker-marker semantics, not the final item scheduler.
    - 2026-05-03 local apply closure item bridge landed: `job_materialization_items(item_kind='local_apply_closure')` now owns pending Phase B/C closure after `inline_incremental_apply` is written. Apply marker writes enqueue deterministic closure items; ingest marker writes complete them; the service loop backfills items from legacy apply-only markers and drains the item queue. `service_metrics.local_apply_backlog` now reports closure item backlog/retry/stale-running counts. This narrows the scan-based fallback without rewriting provider scheduling.
    - 2026-05-03 discovery/provider queue design landed in `DISCOVERY_PROVIDER_QUEUE_CONTRACT.md`: pending discovery rows should become `search_seed_discovery_query` durable items in the existing job item store, provider workers are envelopes only, and `provider_search_retry` remains terminal reporting rather than a standalone retry drain. First implementation slice writes the query item around `_execute_query_spec`; second slice moves Harvest retry-wait/exhausted ownership and non-worker-owned retry drain onto the same item model; historical backfill restores items for legacy workers; worker completion events now close/create discovery items before local apply closure; completed-workflow search-seed worker-summary merge is now migration-only and returns an owner-required skip. Next bounded slice is adding scripted late-webhook/out-of-order gates and then renaming/deleting legacy direct-repair helpers that can still be called from tests/manual repair.
  - [x] Promote materialization streaming budget from sync payload details into a first-class provider-case-report rollup for structured materialization events.
  - [x] Upgrade local scripted smoke to reproduce the OpenAI Infra lane-skew shape (`c5248ea4b3b4`) with a valid zero-result current lane, former-only delta, coherent Stage 1 denominator, and retryable/timeout provider behavior.
  - [x] Upgrade remaining before-ECS service gates for Meta Agent full-reuse rendered timeline and partial delta row streaming. Optional browser-observation variants can still be added for Google Veo/Nano authoritative coverage, late webhook/watcher fallback, slow tail, retryable timeout, tiny-tail coalescing, and writer contention, but those are already covered by local scripted hard gates.
- Acceptance gates:
  - [ ] A terminal workflow cannot expose stable `results` while serving stale baseline-only or raw delta-only board state.
  - [ ] Public APIs stay fast and never synchronously rebuild large artifacts on `/progress`, `/dashboard`, or `/candidates`.
  - [ ] Browser reports fail on snapshot divergence, progress regression, materialized-ahead-of-fetched, stale complete, actor-slot underuse while eligible work exists, unexplained/unmerged tiny batches, and wrong execution-stage wording.
  - [ ] Every implementation slice states which old derived source/fallback it removes, which durable state it introduces, and which negative scripted/browser assertion would have caught the historical failure.

### Excel intake workflow serving and recovery

- Status: first result-view serving fix landed on 2026-04-29 after the 17:10 OpenAI/Google/Meta Excel upload retry.
- Fixed:
  - Excel workflow no longer runs full normalized artifact rebuilds inside `ExcelIntakeService.ingest_contacts()` before writing completed/progress/result-view state.
  - The synchronous path now merges resolved rows into `candidate_documents.json`, marks `artifact_build_deferred=true`, and serves the board through a job-scoped result-view overlay.
  - LinkedIn URL identity matching remains first: Excel company fields can be stale/wrong, and URL/slug identity hits should not be vetoed by uploaded company text.
  - Excel result views now attach a job-scoped `本次Excel导入` candidate marker, and the frontend recall filter can isolate rows matched or created by the current workbook without polluting global candidate assets.
  - Excel batch group `导入目标候选人` / `导出候选人包` buttons are enabled after the backend import contract was scoped to the current job marker.
  - Completed Excel batch groups render matched / manual-review / unresolved row counts from `excel_intake_progress` on `/progress`, not from browser sidecar reads or timeline-text parsing.
- Remaining durable follow-up:
  - [x] Complete and re-enable Excel batch group target-candidate import/export actions:
    - 2026-05-03 implementation: `import_target_candidates_from_job` resolves the `excel_intake:current_job` marker from the canonical result-view/summary contract and only upserts those candidate IDs, never the full company asset population.
    - 2026-05-03 implementation: export by `job_id/history_id` packages the same job-scoped imported target candidates and includes `target_candidates_manifest.json` with export scope, marker lineage, and matched Excel row lineage; regression coverage proves baseline candidates from the same company snapshot are excluded.
    - 2026-05-03 implementation: browser E2E support now clicks import/export and verifies the import message plus downloaded target-candidates archive filename.
  - [x] Preserve compact unresolved/manual-review workbook row details during Excel intake so the export manifest can include more than aggregate unresolved counts.
    - 2026-05-03 implementation: completed Excel workflows persist a bounded `*.excel_intake_row_manifest.json` sidecar with matched rows, review/unresolved rows, status counts, and truncation metadata. Job summary stores only the sidecar reference/counts, and target-candidate ZIP export embeds the sidecar contents under `excel_intake`.
    - 2026-05-03 implementation: `/progress` now exposes a compact `excel_intake_progress` counter contract backed by those manifest counts, and browser E2E asserts the completed batch-group UI displays matched/manual-review/unresolved row counts.
  - [ ] If very large Excel uploads need full raw workbook-row export, add a separate operator/export mode that streams from the original intake artifacts instead of expanding the target-candidate package manifest.
  - [x] Persist each queued group's `prepared_contact_batch` path in the job execution bundle so Excel intake jobs can be resumed after backend process death.
  - [x] Add a watchdog/recovery path for stale `excel_intake` jobs that have a persisted prepared batch, instead of leaving them forever `running/acquiring`.
    - 2026-05-03 implementation: `run_worker_recovery_once` reports `excel_intake_recovery`; eligible jobs are `queued/running acquiring`, have no `intake_id`, and carry a readable prepared batch path in `execution_bundle.excel_intake`.
  - [x] Add an explicit background materialization/admin repair entrypoint for deferred Excel artifact rebuilds, with progress events distinct from the user-visible result-view readiness.
    - 2026-05-03 implementation: completed `excel_intake` jobs with `artifact_build_deferred=true` enqueue/drain the existing `snapshot_full_materialization` durable item kind. `repair-excel-intake-artifacts` defaults to dry-run, `--apply` persists the item, and `--run-now` claims/processes one item immediately. Job events use `event_family=excel_artifact_materialization`, so compaction progress is separate from result-view readiness.
  - [x] Make the Excel execution timeline labels avoid the generic `Public Web Stage 2` wording when the stage is actually local company-asset archival.
    - 2026-05-03 implementation: `execution_phase_contract` treats `excel_intake` / `excel_intake_batch` as local archival, keeps `public_web_stage_applicable=false`, and emits the `公司资产归档` stage override for `/dashboard` and `/progress`. The reused-completed frontend timeline fallback now also receives `workflowKind` so Excel history recovery does not reintroduce the public-web label.

### Live smoke ChatGPT delta progress and worker scheduling regressions

- Status: first-pass result-view/progress/UI-observability fixes landed on 2026-04-29 for live query `我想要OpenAI在ChatGPT组的人`, history `e1ed3b16-ae33-4e97-a2ed-c4e9e29388fc`, job `6fccb376681e`, snapshot `20260429T174612`. The 2026-04-30 hard-browser follow-up is green for OpenAI Agent baseline+delta, OpenAI ChatGPT baseline+delta, and Lovable 100+ live roster.
- Why this is high priority:
  - The run exposed real user-facing instability in execution progress, candidate board hydration, result-view repointing, and profile-scraper scheduling under a large OpenAI scoped delta.
  - This case must be added to scripted/live-observation regression coverage before more Delta asset board streaming changes.
- Fixed in first pass:
  - final `candidate_source` / current workflow snapshot now wins over stale Stage 1 baseline files when repairing `job_result_views`; the live job repairs to `20260429T174612 / 1061` instead of `20260428T183413 / 890`
  - profile-detail progress now reports the actual profile-scraper batch scope once profile workers exist; the live job stabilizes at search returned `166 current + 84 former = 250 deduped`, profile batch required/fetched `165/165`
  - `result_view_lifecycle.baseline_candidate_count` is inferred from the Stage 1 baseline preview, so the frontend no longer guesses baseline size from `expected-required`
  - reused snapshot stage-summary runtime fields are ignored when they predate the current job, preventing old Stage 1 timestamps from being displayed as this job's progress
  - candidate sync now uses lifecycle served/expected counts, not frontend page hydration length, so `候选人同步` should not regress `890 -> 256 -> 96`
  - browser observer now reports snapshot divergence, progress counter regressions, materialization-stuck-after-fetch samples, parsed candidate-sync counts, and backend lifecycle snapshot IDs
  - dashboard `asset_population.candidate_source` now reflects the actually served result view, so browser sampling does not compare a baseline-serving board against a stale/current job summary pointer and miss `job_result_view` divergence
  - live-roster `linkedin_stage_1_progress` now counts completed `harvest_company_employees` roster rows as current returned candidates, not only search-seed/profile-search rows
  - heavy browser coverage now runs both OpenAI ChatGPT baseline+delta and Lovable 100+ live roster, and Lovable asserts the execution-progress samples observe at least `100` current returned rows
  - `execution_phase_contract` is now exposed on `/dashboard` and `/progress`, and the frontend uses it for visible stage titles/details instead of falling back to stale `Public Web Stage 2` wording.
  - candidate dashboard hydration now preserves refreshed lifecycle/progress through page merges and polls active delta/materialization result views at 1s; stable asset views keep the normal 5s interval.
  - delta asset overlay ordering now keeps baseline members in baseline order and appends true delta additions, so a temporary overlay first page does not jump after final materialization.
  - browser observer now fails only when stale `候选人同步 baseline/baseline` exceeds a 2.5s budget after backend exposes delta expected/pending; this keeps the test sensitive to real UI stalls without failing on one-frame API/UI sampling skew.
- Findings to fix:
  - [x] Execution timeline stage semantics:
    - 2026-04-30 fix: `/dashboard` and `/progress` now carry `execution_phase_contract` with `active_phase_id`, `active_stage_id`, `active_phase_label`, `active_phase_detail`, `public_web_stage_applicable`, and stage overrides.
    - Frontend progress mapping prefers this contract, so LinkedIn acquisition, local asset materialization, post-result layering, and final results have phase-specific labels instead of reusing `Public Web Stage 2`.
    - Hard browser assertions now fail on premature Public Web labels while profile work/materialization is pending.
  - [ ] Progress counters need the remaining materialization/apply split:
    - observed current/former/deduped counts moved through `0/0/0`, `2/2/4`, and later `166/84/250`
    - `经去重需取回 LinkedIn Profile` moved between `130`, `176`, `152`, and `250`
    - `已取回 LinkedIn Profile` regressed/oscillated (`86` then `66`) and later jumped to `250`
    - first pass separates deduped search results from actual profile-scraper batch scope; remaining counters should explicitly expose applied-to-snapshot and materialized-to-board counts
    - 2026-04-30 follow-up: `WORKFLOW_PROGRESS_CONTRACT.md` now defines the source-of-truth fields and invariants; Stage 1 progress restores aggregate plus current/former lane search-seed assets, so partial aggregate files should no longer produce `0 current + 74 former` with `174` required profiles. Browser observation now fails on `profile_fetch_required_count > max(deduped_candidate_count, deduped_profile_url_count)`.
    - frontend candidate-board header now uses lifecycle-backed candidate sync, not browser-local candidate page cache length, and polling continues after `results` until delta profile materialization reaches the required count.
    - counters should be split into immutable search-returned totals, deduped profile-url totals, fetched profile count, applied-to-snapshot count, and materialized-to-board count
    - scripted test should assert counters are monotonic within each lane and never mix baseline asset counts with current delta counts
    - 2026-04-30 ECS Infra live follow-up: job `c5248ea4b3b4` (`帮我找OpenAI做Infra方向的人`, snapshot `20260430T155907`) reproduced the same non-atomic progress issue in hosted production. The user saw `新取回在职候选人0 / 新取回离职候选人10 / 需补取 LinkedIn Profile77`, but the final snapshot files showed `current/entries=83`, `former/entries=10`, and deduped `89`. Root cause is still mixed progress sources: lane counts came from partially restored search-seed entries while the profile denominator had already advanced from worker/prefetch URL state. Add this job shape to scripted/hosted assertions: visible current/former/deduped/profile-required counts must come from one consistent progress snapshot, or worker-derived queued URLs must be displayed as a separate technical queue metric.
    - [x] Fixture realism first pass: scripted Harvest rules now support `sample_body_path`; OpenAI ChatGPT and Lovable fixtures replay real prior live provider samples for profile-search/company-employees/profile-scraper before falling back to generated rows. This gives browser/manual scripted runs real locations, experience, education, profile-richness variation, duplicate URLs, and canonical LinkedIn slug behavior. Remaining: OpenAI Agent still needs a large true-Agent live sample if the manual driver must stay on `帮我找OpenAI做Agent方向的人` instead of the ChatGPT live-smoke replay.
  - [x] Candidate board lifecycle/repoint scripted/browser enforcement:
    - UI observed `候选人同步 890/1059`, then `256/1059`, then `96/1059`, while profile progress kept changing
    - Postgres after completion shows job summary candidate source at `20260429T174612` with `1061` candidates, but `job_result_views` still pointed at baseline `20260428T183413` via `recovered_from=current_workflow_snapshot`
    - Fixes now cover stale result-view repair order, frontend page-hydration regressions, lifecycle/progress preservation during dashboard page merges, and stable baseline-first overlay ordering.
    - Browser tests sample board candidate counts, candidate page pagination, result-view snapshot_id, job summary candidate_source, and lifecycle together, and fail on snapshot divergence, progress regression, stale complete sync beyond budget, or materialized-ahead-of-fetched.
    - 2026-04-30 Health live smoke follow-up: fixed an additional drift source where a baseline-serving `job_result_view` could make `_load_workflow_stage_summaries()` read old snapshot stage files before current snapshot repoint completed. Stage-summary resolution now prefers current workflow summary/acquisition/materialization snapshot hints before result-view fallback, completed-summary merge filters stage/runtime fields by primary snapshot id, and lifecycle baseline counts ignore stale stage1 preview baseline explanations from other snapshots.
    - 2026-04-30 Whisper hosted follow-up: added a bounded repair path for completed baseline+delta jobs whose `job_result_view` was accidentally persisted as raw delta-only asset population. Public reads now repair this into a job-scoped baseline+delta overlay when `delta_baseline_snapshot_id` is present and baseline count exceeds the served delta count; direct finalization also falls back to the same overlay if durable generation patch creation is unavailable. Public read repair must stay lightweight and must not invoke candidate artifact materialization fallback; it may only compose an overlay from already-existing baseline/delta `candidate_documents`.
    - 2026-04-30 terminal lease follow-up: completed-workflow reconcile can release a terminal job lease after heartbeat staleness, not only after the long TTL expires or PID death, so stale materialization/repoint leases do not block result-view repair for many minutes.
    - 2026-04-30 ECS Infra live follow-up: job `c5248ea4b3b4` reached `completed/completed` while `job_result_views` still only had the initial baseline-serving view (`snapshot_id=20260430T130836`), and public lifecycle stayed `baseline_serving` with `expected_candidate_count=1200`, `delta_profile_required/fetched=89/89`, but `delta_profile_materialized_count=19`. `/dashboard` and `/candidates` timed out for 20-30s during this state. This is a stronger reproduction of completion-before-repoint: final job completion must not be visible as stable `results` until result-view lifecycle has either published a current/overlay serving view or explicitly reports a bounded post-result materialization state that the candidate page can serve without synchronous rebuild/read-repair.
    - Remaining architecture follow-up: make `result_view_lifecycle` an atomic persisted job lifecycle record instead of assembling baseline/current/served/progress/materialization from job summary, result-view metadata, and candidate-source payloads.
      - Add a `job_result_lifecycle` or equivalent persisted record keyed by `job_id` / `view_id` that owns `baseline_snapshot_id`, `current_snapshot_id`, `served_snapshot_id`, `expected_candidate_count`, `served_candidate_count`, delta fetch/materialization counters, and lifecycle phase.
      - Make workflow completion, baseline-serving publication, current materialization, result-view repoint, progress API, dashboard API, and candidate page API write/read this record through one backend path.
      - Stop re-deriving lifecycle state from mixed `summary_payload`, `job_result_views.metadata`, `asset_population.candidate_source`, and stage-summary files except as repair input with explicit source/snapshot validation.
      - Add a regression that reproduces a baseline-serving result view plus later current snapshot repoint and asserts every public API observes the same lifecycle row at each transition.
  - [x] Materialization and reconcile repeated too aggressively:
    - completed-workflow reconcile emitted repeated `harvest_prefetch started/materialize_deferred/materialize_completed` sequences for the same worker set
    - materialization started before all profile workers drained, then deferred repeatedly; later snapshot materialization and outreach layering also ran under completed-job reconcile
    - 2026-04-29 fix: completed-job inline callback reconcile now uses a cross-process `completed_workflow_reconcile:{job_id}:{kind}` in-flight slot, and callback reconcile re-collects unconsumed workers after acquiring the slot. Duplicate concurrent callbacks now emit a structured `phase=coalesced` skip instead of entering materialize/retrieval again.
    - Materialize signatures now include worker IDs, and scripted smoke expectations can fail on `max_repeated_materialize_signature_count` / `max_same_worker_reconcile_repeat_count`.
    - OpenAI ChatGPT scripted validation now passes with `repeated_materialize_signature_count=0`, `same_worker_reconcile_repeat_count=0`, and only one completed `harvest_prefetch` materialize call after same-kind profile workers drain.

Set `SOURCING_LOCAL_PROVIDER_EVENT_WATCH_WITH_WEBHOOK_ENABLED=0` only for strict external-webhook roundtrip smoke.

This closes the false `true active provider slots=4` / `remote actor worker count=1` reading caused by sleeping before remote identifiers existed.

Observed actor batches were `8`, then `33/34`, then a small cached `3`; provider limiter `budget=4` but active count only reached about `2`. The first batch was only former-side URLs; current search entries (`83`) were not fully visible until later. Completion events show local watcher often beat provider webhook, and callback-side profile prefetch/cache-marker confirmation blocked for about `295-302s` with `dispatched_url_count=0`. Add metrics/guards for `remote_completed -> local event`, `local event -> next submit`, `profile_prefetch cache-marker wait`, and `actor budget occupancy over time`; the scheduler should continue filling available slots from already-known queued URLs instead of waiting on cache marker confirmation inside the same handoff path.

### Result population contract for directional/scoped reuse

- Status: deferred by user on 2026-04-28; do not implement in the Infra/frontend display patch.
- Problem to solve next:
  - The system records `directional_query`, shard registry coverage, and `source_matches`, but it does not yet treat "this request expects a scoped shard population" as a first-class contract through reuse policy.
  - `authoritative` / `latest` serving snapshot is a pointer and reuse hint, not proof that the snapshot is a strict full-company roster. Google/OpenAI-style companies can have authoritative scoped/canonical views with many shards, but that does not automatically satisfy a new unrelated Infra/Coding shard.
  - For directional queries, exact shard/query coverage can satisfy reuse; unrelated scoped snapshots should not masquerade as full-population baselines.
- Expected future shape:
  - Add an explicit request/result-population field, e.g. `result_population_contract=scoped_shard_population | company_canonical_population`.
  - Thread it through request preview, semantic brief, acquisition strategy, asset reuse planning, dispatch explanation, and frontend plan labels.
  - Reuse policy should distinguish `authoritative serving pointer`, `explicit full-company baseline proof`, and `exact scoped shard coverage`.
- Current bounded fix already landed:
  - 2026-05-02: `requested_population_boundary` and coverage-proof-aware planning are live; directional queries with all-members wording use full-company local filtering only when full-company coverage is proven, otherwise they remain scoped directional.
  - 2026-05-03: `infra` remains an explicit `infra_systems` alias for candidate evidence and role/facet matching, but a thematic/default `Infra` signal no longer forces `infra_systems` into hard request role buckets without explicit role evidence. Frontend keyword display filters role-bucket tokens from "检索关键词".
  - 2026-05-03: broad default technical categories such as `researcher`/`engineer` stay soft retrieval hints when they originate from structured/default technical provenance, so they cannot hide valid leads or infrastructure heads during relaxed/manual-review retrieval.
  - 2026-05-03: board/progress contracts recognize `asset_population` as a first-class serving mode; do not regress to `results`-only assertions or ranked-results-only served counts.

### Delta asset board streaming contract

- Status: lifecycle split first pass implemented and hosted/browser hard-mode validation passed on 2026-04-29; 2026-04-30 heavy browser gate is green for OpenAI Agent baseline+delta, OpenAI ChatGPT baseline+delta, and Lovable live roster with stage-label, sync-lag, snapshot-divergence, progress-regression, materialized-ahead-of-fetched, and overlay-order guardrails.
- Observed job: `754cedf840c8` (`帮我找OpenAI在Pulse组的人`, snapshot `20260428T183413`).
- Previous behavior:
  - Stage 1 and Harvest profile tail were partly event-level: search seed acquisition queued a Harvest profile prefetch worker, remote webhook arrived at `18:34:41`, and local provider-tail handling wrote the apply marker at `18:34:44`.
  - End-to-end board serving was not yet delta-streamed. The dashboard waited for a job-scoped current snapshot/result view backed by materialized `asset_population` artifacts, so the user saw the board only after full 890-person artifact/retrieval/result-view work was far enough along.
  - For this job, the provider work was fast; the slow path was local asset work: background harvest-prefetch reconcile/materialize started around `18:36:48`, full snapshot normalization/retrieval completed around `18:41:37`, then outreach layering attached around `18:41:54`.
- Desired behavior:
  - When a reusable baseline exists, publish a temporary job result view immediately against the baseline snapshot/generation, e.g. `baseline_snapshot_id=20260428T094936`, with an explicit delta overlay state.
  - Frontend should show the baseline population immediately, with separate progress such as `baseline_loaded=888/890` or `baseline_profile_fetched=888/890`, plus `delta_pending=2` and `delta_fetched=0..2`.
  - When the delta snapshot is locally applied and materialized, merge or repoint to the current snapshot/generation and update the same board to `890/890`, without waiting to hide the baseline board behind full current-snapshot finalization.
  - Outreach/layering should expose its own post-result status so the board can refresh layer metadata when it arrives, instead of relying on manual refresh after completed-job reconcile.
- Contract work needed:
  - Build the scripted/user-interaction test foundation first; do not implement this against the lightweight OpenAI/Pulse two-profile delta because that case is too small to expose realistic streaming/materialization risks.
  - Use an older OpenAI baseline plus a larger Agent scoped-search delta as the canonical scripted driver: profile-search current/former should model probe + scaled calls, return hundreds of synthetic candidates with duplicates, then queue batched profile-scraper workers under an actor-slot cap before running real local delta snapshot/artifact/materialization/finalization in the isolated runtime.
  - The scripted harness must simulate provider content, timing, ordering, and edge cases: zero-result retry for the same payload, partial/scaled result degradation, out-of-order current/former completion, profile scraper retryable pending rounds, late webhook/recovery, and materialization slow path.
  - Browser/user-interaction assertions are part of the feature, not optional: assert baseline board visibility before current-snapshot materialization, visible delta progress, actor-slot occupancy, no DataForSEO Stage 1 fallback, no duplicate provider dispatch, stable pagination/hydration, automatic layer refresh, and final result-view repoint/merge.
  - Real Harvest/API calls are not part of the default development loop. After scripted coverage is green, leave the final low-cost live smoke as an explicit user-run manual gate.
  - [x] Add a first-class `result_view_lifecycle` / `delta_overlay_state` contract distinguishing `baseline_serving`, `delta_applying`, `current_snapshot_materializing`, `current_snapshot_serving`, and `post_result_layering`.
  - [x] Persist the initial baseline result view during workflow start when a `delta_baseline_snapshot_id` is available.
  - [x] Return baseline + delta progress in `/api/jobs/{job_id}/dashboard`, `/candidates`, and `/progress`; do not encode it only in natural-language timeline text.
  - [x] Add frontend rendering for baseline-serving board plus delta progress.
  - [x] Add hosted/browser hard-mode validation that asserts the board is renderable before current-snapshot materialization completes, then repoints/merges after delta finalization.
  - [x] Add user-visible execution metrics and candidate-sync metrics to the same browser assertion: current/former returned counts, deduped candidate/profile count, profile fetched/required, profile materialized-to-board, stale-complete sync budget, and retryable failures.
  - [ ] Add a second-stage delta board streaming path where completed profile batches become board-visible incrementally instead of holding `delta_profile_materialized_count=0` until the full current snapshot/result view repoints. This should preserve the existing board-visible contract but add a partial delta overlay/materialization channel so the user can consume profiles as batches finish.
  - [ ] Make LinkedIn Stage 1 progress atomic across search-seed lane counts and profile-fetch denominator. The current progress builder can briefly show states such as `current=0, former=74, deduped/profile_required=174` because lane counts come from search-seed entries while the denominator is also supplemented from queued profile-worker URLs. The UI should not present mixed-source snapshots as a coherent lane breakdown. Fix direction must choose an explicit contract: either advance `profile_fetch_required_count` only from the same lane/search snapshot used for `current/former/deduped`, or expose worker-derived URLs separately as `known_profile_urls_from_workers` / `queued_profile_url_count` instead of mixing them into the business-facing search-returned totals.
  - [ ] Verify automatic layer metadata refresh after `outreach_layering.status` changes in hosted/browser hard mode.
- Foundation started:
  - Added `configs/scripted/openai_agent_scoped_delta_streaming.json` as the first canonical OpenAI Agent long-tail scoped-delta fixture. It models an older OpenAI baseline, current/former Harvest profile-search probe+scale, duplicate profile URLs, profile-scraper retryable pending rounds, and no default DataForSEO/public-web Stage 1 fallback.

This is required because Delta board streaming must test baseline+delta serving behavior, not a delta-only artifact.

### Event-level workflow design review handoff

- 2026-04-28 已把流式响应级 workflow 的业务预期、状态机边界、真实踩坑和 review checklist 固化到 `docs/EVENT_LEVEL_WORKFLOW_RESPONSE.md`。
- 已新增 `docs/CLAUDE_CODE_EVENT_WORKFLOW_REVIEW_PROMPT.md`，用于启动 Claude Code 新 session 做工程 review。
- 2026-04-28 review pass 1 (running-job 路径) + pass 2 (completed-job 路径 + 双 marker 拆分) + pass 3 (真正的 marker recoverability + Phase B 失败短路) + pass 4 (closed two pre-existing pipeline failures) + pass 5 (H4 query dedupe + H5 source_matches recall + provider-slot idle metric) 已合入：
  - [x] **C1** lock contention — running-job (`_process_inline_incremental_worker_batch`) 和 completed-job (`_reconcile_completed_workflow_after_company_roster` / `_after_search_seed` / `_after_harvest_prefetch`) 都已拆为 Phase A (lock: collect + apply + provisional apply marker)、Phase B (outside lock: profile prefetch / next-submit)、Phase C (outside lock: full materialize + retrieval + outreach_layering + final ingest marker)。harvest_prefetch 既有 pre-writer prefetch hop 保留不动
  - [x] **M2** registry-only marker path — running-job + completed-job 路径下的 company_roster / search_seed prefetch 调用均显式传 `load_cached_profile_payloads=False`；wrapper `_queue_background_profile_prefetch_from_search_seed_snapshot` 已固化该参数
  - [x] **真正的 marker recoverability**（pass 3 才完整闭环）— `inline_incremental_apply` 与 `inline_incremental_ingest` 拆开。Phase A 在 lock 内写 `inline_incremental_apply`（不阻塞 collector）；Phase C **真正完成** (`sync_result.status in {completed, skipped, deferred}`) 后才写 `inline_incremental_ingest`（继续是 collector 的 gate）。Pass 2 还残留的“materializing/deferred 占位 ingest marker”在 completed-job 三条路径全部移除；running-job 的 final per-worker ingest 写入也已 gate 在 sync 终态上
  - [x] **Phase B 失败短路**（pass 3）— 新增 `_phase_b_prefetch_indicates_retryable_failure`；running-job 路径和 completed-job 三条路径都会在 Phase B prefetch `status=failed` 且 `retryable=True` 时直接 return `status=prefetch_failed_retryable`，不进入 Phase C，也不写 ingest marker。worker 只保留 apply marker，下一轮 recovery 自然重捡
  - [x] **search_seed `apply_already_consumed` 状态恢复**（pass 3）— 当 apply marker 已覆盖当前 snapshot 时，合成 apply_result 现在通过 `_restore_search_seed_snapshot_from_snapshot_dir` 恢复 `search_seed_snapshot`，否则下一轮 retry prefetch 会以 `search_seed_snapshot_missing` 静默跳过

### Company canonical serving view vs job result view boundary

- 2026-04-28 复盘 OpenAI / Google scoped artifact rebuild 时明确：
  - `canonical_merged` 目前是单个 snapshot 下的 artifact view，不是自动吞掉所有历史 snapshot 的 evergreen company index
  - `source_matches` / `matched_keywords` 才是“一人多 query/source shard”的表达方式
  - `source_snapshot_selection` 决定 materialization 合并哪些 source snapshots；不能用无边界 all-history union 模拟多 sharding
  - `job_result_view` 是某次 job 的 serving 指针；新 scoped workflow 完成后应自动指向本次 current snapshot
  - 更全的前端 company board 应该消费明确发布的 company canonical serving snapshot/generation，而不是旧 job snapshot 的历史 fallback
- 当前状态：
  - Google/Gemini `92eb11472da4` 已指向 `20260428T011339`；本地 serving artifacts 已生成 `9359` 人、`188` pages，`source_matches/matched_keywords` 记录数为 `3940`，其中 Gemini source hits 为 `43`
  - OpenAI Agent `828b63063fb3` 已有新版 `source_matches` projection
  - OpenAI `20260422T161258` 已恢复为该 job snapshot 边界的 `352` 人，不再保留误写的全历史 `826` 人 artifact
- 后续 backlog：
  - [x] 增加显式 company canonical serving view rebuild/repair 命令：`rebuild-company-serving-view` 输入 snapshot / selected snapshot set，输出新的 generation，并记录 `source_snapshot_selection`、projection version、build profile
  - [x] 增加 job result view repoint policy：`repoint-job-result-view` 区分 `historical_replay` 与 `serve_latest_company_asset`，默认 dry-run，避免 maintenance repair 静默改变旧 job 的语义
  - [x] 增加 audit：`audit-company-serving-view` 列出 job result view 指向的 snapshot、authoritative registry snapshot、artifact `source_snapshot_selection`、projection version、source provenance 采样；scoped jobs 只报告，不自动 repoint

### Event-level apply/materialize split from Google/Gemini 2026-04-28

- 已确认这是工程审查标准缺口：
  - 旧测试只证明单次 callback 的代码顺序，没有覆盖 writer lock 长时间持有时的新 remote completion 是否仍能及时补位
  - 服务级调度验收必须包含 provider slot vacancy / next-submit latency / writer-lock contention，而不是只看最终 artifact correctness
- 当前 contract：
  - next-submit opportunity 必须在 job writer lock 之前执行
  - provider dataset apply 成功后必须立即写 `inline_incremental_ingest`
  - full materialize/retrieval/layering 是 downstream 阶段，必须由 `background_snapshot_materialization` 或结构化 reconcile events 单独恢复
  - completed reconcile 必须先 backfill 旧 summary 已证明 consumed 的 worker markers，再处理新的 pending same-kind worker
  - 大 snapshot 的 Harvest profile apply 不允许 URL miss 后全量 candidate 扫描；必须先用 URL/name index 预筛选
- 真实 Google/Gemini `92eb11472da4` 已修到安全状态：
  - `17/17` Harvest profile workers 已写 marker
  - downstream materialization 后续已通过独立 artifact builder 收完，job result view 已 repoint 到 Google `20260428T011339`
  - 没有继续新发 Harvest provider run
- 后续 backlog：
  - [ ] 把 apply/materialize 分阶段耗时写入结构化 metric：`profile_payload_load_ms`、`candidate_match_ms`、`candidate_doc_write_ms`、`full_materialize_ms`
  - [ ] 给 live smoke 增加锁竞争场景：writer lock 被占用时，新 completion 的 next-submit 必须在阈值内发生
  - [x] 把 completed terminal job 的 stale reconcile lease takeover 单独收口；当前 runner 已死但 job classification=completed 时需要更明确的 safe-release path，而不是人工按 owner/token 释放
  - [ ] 将未来仍处于 `background_snapshot_materialization.status=scheduled` 的 jobs 放到低优先级 maintenance 队列，避免手动测试时自动抢占 CPU

### Google/Gemini Harvest profile-search zero-result instability from 2026-04-28

- 真实 query/job `92eb11472da4` 和 Harvest/API 服务商回复共同确认 Harvest `linkedin-profile-search` provider 边界：
  - 同一 input 偶发 `0 profiles` 是已知 LinkedIn/provider transient issue，需要 bounded retry
  - provider total 在不同 run 间可能大幅变化，例如同一 Gemini/currentCompanies=Google input 曾返回 `0`、`1845`、`2338`
  - 缺页/页段覆盖不足比真 0 更常见，且受 probe total 漂移叠加，不能被当作可靠的 strong incomplete 判据
- 当前 contract：
  - 只有 `rows=[]` 且 `pagination.total_elements<=0` 的真 0 结果会触发 zero-result retry
  - probe、scale、page chunk、single-page fallback 都透传 retry 设置，默认最多重试 `2` 次，可由 `HARVEST_PROFILE_SEARCH_ZERO_RESULT_RETRY_ATTEMPTS` 或 cost policy 覆盖
  - provider people-search scale 空结果会尝试 page chunk fallback
  - chunk 空结果会尝试 single-page retry
  - 如果仍存在空页段或返回数小于 provider total/effective limit，query summary 默认写 `status=degraded`、`provider_search_degraded=true`
  - 只有 zero-result retry 耗尽且没有可用 fallback rows 时，query summary 才写 `status=incomplete`、`provider_search_incomplete=true`
  - scoped-search former lane 必须保留 scoped keyword（例如 `Gemini`），不能退化成 broad past-company-only 查询
- 当前真实 job 处理：
  - Google/Gemini 的 provider incomplete search worker 已按 retry-later/incomplete 边界处理，不再触发 search-seed materialize 或继续花费 provider 调用
  - 现有 serving artifact 仅代表已落地 snapshot 的可服务集合，不代表 provider theoretical total 已全量取回
- 后续 backlog：
  - [x] 增加 provider anomaly ops panel/metric：2026-05-03 local fix: `workflow_service_metrics.provider_anomalies` now reads durable discovery/provider-retry item metadata and reports zero-result retry, exhausted/accepted zero-result, empty scale fallback, probe-total drift/degraded coverage, empty page ranges, and single-page retry counts. `workflow_smoke` exports and aggregates the same service-metric subreport so scripted/browser reports can distinguish provider-supply anomalies from local scheduler/recovery bugs.
  - [x] 给 live/scripted smoke 增加“probe total 与实际 fetched page coverage”检查入口：2026-05-03 local fix: smoke expectations now support explicit maxima for provider anomaly counts. These gates are opt-in so degraded provider coverage is visible by default but only fails cases that declare provider-quality SLOs.
  - [ ] Add at least one live-observation matrix case that declares provider anomaly maxima after ECS/local parity is available; keep normal local scripted cases from failing solely on accepted zero-result scoped lanes.
  - [ ] 对 Google/Gemini 这条历史 job 暂不继续 live repair；除非明确要付费重试，否则保留 provider anomaly event，后续手测可换其他 scoped query

### Event-level efficiency/service-grade runtime hardening

- 当前新增指标：
  - `remote_to_local_event_lag_ms`: provider timestamp 到本地 provider event 记录
  - `remote_to_local_marker_lag_ms`: provider timestamp 到 Harvest completion local marker / next-submit opportunity
  - `local_completion_to_next_submit_start_ms`: 本地 profile completion/apply marker 到 profile-refill owner 下一批 submit 开始；这是纯 scheduler handoff 指标，discovery/roster/search-seed Phase B append submit 不参与该 hard gate
  - `next_submit_provider_attempt_elapsed_ms`: 下一批 submit/profile-submit attempt 的耗时，可能包含 scripted provider sleep/pending 与 discovery append submit work；不能用它代表纯 handoff
  - `local_to_next_submit_start_ms` / `next_submit_attempt_elapsed_ms`: 兼容旧报告的 alias
  - `reconcile.same_worker_reconcile_repeat_count`: 同一 worker 被 completed reconcile 重复消费的次数
  - `reconcile.materialize_call_count` / `reconcile.repeated_materialize_signature_count`: 下游 materialize/rebuild 调用和重复签名
  - `provider_slots.true_active_provider_slot_worker_count`: 持有 limiter lease 的真实 provider slot worker
  - `provider_slots.remote_actor_worker_count`: 已有 run/dataset 或 remote-wait 阶段的 actor worker
  - `provider_slots.pre_submit_provider_worker_count`: 没有 limiter lease 却处于 active/remote-wait 口径的 phantom worker，出现即视为调度错误
- 后续 backlog：
  - [x] 把 `completed_reconcile` lease acquire/skip、marker backfill、full rebuild start/end 写成结构化 job events，减少从自然语言 detail 推断的比例
    - 2026-04-27 更新：新的 metrics 如果看到 `event_family=completed_workflow_reconcile`，会忽略同 job 中残留的 legacy `detail` materialization 事件，避免迁移期重复计数
  - [ ] 给 live smoke 增加环境化 SLO 阈值：ECS webhook 场景可要求 provider completion 后 1-5 秒内出现 local marker；本地 tunnel/debug 场景只输出观测，不默认失败
  - [x] 把 event-level efficiency rollup 暴露到前端/ops runtime panel，显示“provider 已完成但本地 marker/materialize 未追平”的具体 lag，而不是只显示候选人同步计数 — 2026-05-04 `contracts/frontend_runtime_dashboard.example.tsx` 已接入 `/api/runtime/metrics.event_level_efficiency`。
  - [ ] 对 Public Web / DataForSEO lane 复用同一指标口径：remote search completion、local signal write、LLM adjudication、export materialize 需要拆开计数
  - [ ] 对 materialization 热路径增加更精确的 per-phase metric：prepare candidates、timeline/profile lookup、artifact write、PG upsert、layering/export projection，避免只看到一个总耗时

  - `sqlite_snapshot` 已从 UI/CLI/import/object-sync 产品路径退役；后续只能做 PG-native/Public Web 新模块

### 1. Harden `start-workflow` runner-spawn and background recovery

- `start-workflow` 当前虽然已经默认 non-blocking，但 runner-spawn / recovery daemon 仍需要更稳定的 ownership 和重试语义。
- 目标是不依赖当前终端存活，也能让 job 从 `plan_review -> background acquisition -> results` 自己收口。
- 需要把 job-scoped runner / daemon / worker 状态边界继续收敛，减少“主 job 已启动，但后台实际没接上”的不透明状态。

### 2. Run a fresh end-to-end quality pass on top of the stabilized Anthropic/Humans& workflow

- Anthropic 当前已经有可复用的 durable snapshot `20260409T080131`，不应再把“重新抓大组织全量 roster”当成默认验证方式。
- 更合理的下一轮验证应该聚焦：
  - query interpretation
  - retrieval quality
  - ranking / filtering
  - manual review routing
  - result explanation
- `Humans&` 仍然适合作为中等规模真实组织继续做一次完整质量测试。
- Anthropic 更适合作为“大组织增量复用、former fallback、adaptive shard”的长期 regression fixture。

		  - 已补 URL-shape guardrail：X/Substack/GitHub/Scholar 的 deep/post/repo/non-profile 链接即使被 LLM 标 `likely_same_person`，也只作为 evidence/review signal，不进入 `primary_links`，`person_public_web_signals.publishable=false`，detail API/UI 展示 `link_shape_warnings`
		  - 已补效率 guardrail：候选人内 URL fetch 支持 bounded concurrency，实验 CLI 的候选人 finalization/LLM adjudication 支持 bounded concurrency；DataForSEO readiness 现在按 query/task item 处理，`tasks_ready` miss 后会做 bounded `task_get` direct probe，`40601 Task Handed` / `40602 Task In Queue` 作为 provider-pending metadata 记录。超过本地 poll budget 但仍 pending 时只 reset 该 query 的 task id，保留 `previous_task_ids` / `provider_task_reset_reason`，不重发 sibling queries 或整批 run。2026-06-04 live validation 发现 DataForSEO Standard Queue 长尾会超过普通 remote wait：显式 provider-pending 现在使用独立 `max_provider_pending_wait_seconds` hard budget；超过 `max_remote_search_wait_seconds` 但仍为 `40601`/`40602` 时保持 `waiting_remote_search` 并暴露 `provider_pending_wait_deferred`，不再 terminalize 为 `completed_with_errors`。已被旧代码 terminalize 的 historical run 仍需要 force-refresh 或后续新增 `crm.public_web.search.late_result_adopt` typed command 接回；该 adoption 不能由 reader fallback 执行。下一轮 live/product validation 需要在重启后的新进程上确认 `provider_task_reset_count`、`provider_pending_deferred_count` 和最终 materialization 质量。
		  - 2026-06-04 follow-up：3 人 force-refresh run 证明 DataForSEO 搜索阶段已恢复为 item-level success（每人 fetch 10，search timeout 为 0，raw links 已生成）；当前 live validation 阻塞转为模型 provider。`gpt-5.5` 通过 chshapi 的 `/models` 可见，但 `/chat/completions` 返回 `auth_unavailable` 或 `usage_limit_reached`，因此 Public Web adjudication 会 fail-closed fallback。模型 healthcheck 必须验证真实 generation endpoint；artifact、phase metrics 和 detail API 必须暴露 `provider/model/model_version/fallback_reason/model_error`，W7g 不允许用 deterministic fallback 伪装模型 review。2026-06-04 hardening：OpenAI-compatible model calls now use a provider/base-url/model circuit breaker plus cached healthcheck responses. `/api/providers/health`、worker recovery tick、或 Public Web adjudication 失败后不能继续形成非流式 LLM 调用循环；冷却期内必须本地 fail-fast 并暴露 `model_provider_circuit_open`。同日 sharedchat relay 已验证 `https://new.sharedchat.cc/codex` 的 `/responses` 可用且 `gpt-5.5` healthcheck 返回 `MODEL_OK`；本地 normal config 应使用 `model_provider.api_style=openai_responses`，不要把该 relay 误配为 chat completions。
	  - Historical target-candidate detail section used `GET /api/target-candidates/{record_id}/public-web-search`; current normal detail reads `GET /api/crm/records/{crm_record_id}/public-web-search` and promotion must continue through persistent CRM/PersonAssertion APIs, not frontend state or legacy target-candidate rows.
  - Historical Web Search export used `POST /api/target-candidates/public-web-export`; current normal export uses `POST /api/crm/records/public-web-export`, default `promoted_only`, and continues to exclude raw HTML/PDF/search payload.
  - Historical email/profile link promotion used `GET/POST /api/target-candidates/{record_id}/public-web-promotions`; current normal promotion uses `GET/POST /api/crm/records/{crm_record_id}/public-web-promotions` and writes `PersonAssertion` + CRM event. Legacy `target_candidates.primary_email` mutation is migration bridge behavior only.
  - non-publishable 或 dirty URL-shape signal 的人工覆盖确认已接入；必须填写 `override_reason`，detail/export 会保留原始 validation reason 和 override reason，hard invalid email 不可覆盖
  - Public Web target-candidate frontend browser E2E 已覆盖 selection -> trigger -> polling/refresh -> compact card status，并补充 detail promotion -> `promoted_and_publishable` export 下载链路；后续可扩展 override prompt 的 browser 断言
  - 目标候选人页已补 toolbar/filter 布局、卡片编辑保存和详情抽屉收口：Public Web Search 与刷新状态靠近、LinkedIn/Web Search 导出改为范围语义、说明文字进入问号 tooltip、跟进状态和 Public Web 状态支持多选筛选；跟进状态/质量分/备注现在是显式草稿保存；公开信息详情、证据 review 和 promotion 进入页面级 drawer，不再撑开卡片 grid。
  - 下一步先手动验证当前 override/export/profile mode：重启 backend/worker，对 2-3 个目标候选人跑 Public Web Search，分别检查 composed profile、普通确认、覆盖确认、`人工确认` 导出和 `含高置信` 导出。代码层后续转为 live quality tuning for company Public Web source discovery；company-level API/CLI-only、collector_bundle contract/live collector source fetcher/discovery、scoped polling、retry/cancel、显式卡片编辑保存状态和目标候选人详情抽屉已落地

### 3. Productize asset governance and snapshot promotion

- 第一层 `draft / partial / canonical / superseded / archived / empty` lifecycle gate 已落成可执行规则：
  - `canonical` 可参与 promotion / authoritative baseline
  - `draft / partial / superseded / archived / empty` 可补充资产，但不可自动成为 coverage baseline
- `2026-04-25` 已新增 default pointer / canonical replacement helper：
  - `src/sourcing_agent/asset_governance.py`
  - default pointer key 为 `(company_key, scope_kind, scope_key, asset_kind)`
  - replacement plan 会显式返回新 canonical pointer、旧 superseded pointer、historical retention 摘要
  - 非 canonical lifecycle 不允许成为 default pointer
- 后续要继续做的是治理产品化，而不是再补 promotion 分支：
- company asset / scoped asset default pointer 的持久化 writer 已完成，后续不要再让调用方手工改 `latest_snapshot.json`。
- 下一步应做 governance UI / review flow，以及 promotion-time 自动 stamping `coverage_proof`，而不是恢复旧的隐式 authoritative promotion。
- 继续扩充“可用于排序和分析的长期 roster 资产”，优先考虑：

  - roster 在 X / Substack 上的公开内容资产

  - 从个人主页和论文附录中抽取 email 时的 lineage / evidence 落盘规则
